{near|between|under|during} the sea. :: 海の近くの
{Behind|Before|Since|Until} her house, :: 彼女の家の裏に
there {is|are|be|has} a large garden. :: 大きな庭がある
Every {summer|summers|winters|years}, :: 毎年夏に
I {visit|visits|visiting|am visit} her :: 私は彼女を訪ねる
and {help|helps|helping|to helped} her :: そして彼女を手伝う
{in|of|to|from} the garden. :: 庭で
We {grow|grows|growing|is grow} tomatoes, :: 私たちはトマトを育てる
{cucumbers|cucumber|a cucumber|cucumbering}, :: キュウリ
and many {kinds|kind|kindness|kinded} of flowers. :: たくさんの種類の花を
{Working|Worked|Works|To working} in the garden :: 庭で働くことは
{is|are|be|being} hard, :: 大変だ
{especially|special|especial|specialty} on hot days. :: 特に暑い日には

always {says|say|saying|said to}, :: いつも言う

{At first|At last|At least|At once}, :: 最初は
I {didn't|don't|wasn't|haven't} understand :: 私は理解できなかった
{what|that|which|who} she meant. :: 彼女が何を言いたいのか
{Last|Next|Ago|Later} year, :: 去年
I {planted|plant|planting|plants} some seeds :: 私はいくつかの種をまいた
{by myself|by me|for mine|of myself} :: 自分ひとりで
and {waited|waiting|waits|to wait} for weeks. :: そして何週間も待った
{When|What|Which|Who} the first small leaves :: 最初の小さな葉が
finally {appeared|appear|appearing|appears}, :: ついに現れたとき

{that|what|which|who} good things take time. :: 良いことは時間がかかると

beautiful {for|to|by|at} her. :: 彼女のために美しく
